I couldn't sit still at all. I kept jumping up from my desk to sketch out new office layouts, or I'd start reorganizing all my files. It's just been steady like this the whole two months.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any periods where you felt nearly normal, but then your symptoms worsened again?\n\nPatient: No, honestly, it's just like I was saying... there haven't been any ups and downs like that. It's just been a flat, steady low this entire time.\n\nDoctor: Would you describe your current low mood and associated symptoms as mild, moderate, or severe?\n\nPatient: I would say it's moderate.
